However, existing benchmarks predominantly focus on simplified Text-to-SQL translation or data analysis, leaving the critical and complex domain of end-to-end data engineering largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce DataClawEval, the first comprehensive benchmark designed specifically to evaluate the end-to-end task completion capabilities of autonomous agents in real-world data engineering scenarios. Built upon production-grade code authored by professional enterprise data engineers, it comprises 100 rigorous, end-to-end tasks spanning five execution engines: PySpark, MySQL, HiveSQL, PrestoSQL/Trino, and FlinkSQL. Rather than non-deterministic LLM-as-a-judge scoring, each task is executed within a case-specific, isolated sandbox and graded by deterministic, rule-based scripts. Evaluating 16 frontier agents exposes critical limitations: The strongest model attains only 74.9 overall, and no single model dominates, as each excels on a different engine, revealing strict domain specialization rather than omnipotent proficiency. Thus, autonomous data engineering remains a formidable, unresolved challenge. We release our dataset, containerized environments, and deterministic evaluation scripts at https://github.com/Dicemy/DataClawEval/tree/master

</details>


### [250] [SKILL-KD: Contrastive Skill Distillation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28048)
*Qiming Shi,Yibo Dou,Jiawen Zhu,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Zhaolu Kang,Yunfan Zhou,Di Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SKILL-KD，一种对比性技能蒸馏框架，将技能作为强弱不同的智能体之间的显式蒸馏媒介。给定学生失败轨迹和教师轨迹，SKILL-KD将可操作的差异蒸馏为文本技能补丁，通过重新运行学生来评估补丁，并在失败时迭代细化。为防止技能漂移，框架维护轨迹关联的编辑历史并进行漂移感知技能整合，决定每个补丁应新增规则、删除/修改现有规则还是跳过。在五个智能体基准和两种学生设置下，SKILL-KD持续优于固定模型自适应基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能获取方法通常将技能视为经验摘要、记忆条目或成功演示的直接总结，这导致能力较弱的学生智能体在缺乏任务知识或操作策略时无法从失败轨迹中推断缺失行为，而教师轨迹又过于隐式难以内化为可复用的指导，存在能力不匹配的问题。

Method: SKILL-KD是一种对比性技能蒸馏框架，核心流程包括：(1) 给定学生失败和教师对同一任务的轨迹，将两者可操作的差异蒸馏为文本技能补丁；(2) 通过重新运行学生来评估补丁有效性；(3) 若学生仍失败则迭代细化补丁；(4) 维护轨迹关联的编辑历史，通过漂移感知技能整合（Drift-Aware Skill Consolidation）决定每个补丁是新增规则、删除或修改现有规则，还是跳过，以防止局部更新导致的技能漂移。

Result: 在五个智能体基准和两种学生设置下，SKILL-KD一致地提升了冻结学生智能体的性能，优于固定模型自适应基线。

Conclusion: 将技能作为显式蒸馏媒介，通过对比性差异提取和漂移感知的技能整合，能够有效地在不同能力的智能体之间传递可操作的知识，从而提升弱学生智能体的任务表现。

Abstract: Skill-based prompting has become a practical mechanism for improving large language model (LLM) agents, yet existing skill acquisition methods often treat skills as experience summaries, memory entries, or direct summaries of successful demonstrations. This creates a mismatch for weaker student agents: when a student fails because it lacks task knowledge or operational strategy, its failed trajectory may not contain enough evidence to infer the missing behavior, while the teacher trajectory may be too implicit to be internalized as reusable guidance. We propose SKILL-KD, a contrastive skill distillation framework that treats skills as an explicit distillation medium between agents of different capabilities. Given a student failure and the teacher trajectory on the same task, SKILL-KD distills their actionable discrepancy into a textual skill patch, evaluates the patch by re-running the student, and iteratively refines the patch when the student still fails. To prevent repeated local updates from causing skill drift, SKILL-KD further maintains trace-linked edit histories and performs Drift-Aware Skill Consolidation, deciding whether each patch should add a new rule, delete or modify an existing rule, or be skipped. Across five agent benchmarks and two student settings, SKILL-KD consistently improves frozen student agents over fixed-model adaptation baselines.

</details>


### [251] [SciDataSailor: Deep Scientific Data Exploring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28098)
*Jiyong Rao,Yicheng Qiu,Chi Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Runkai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Deep Scientific Data Exploration（深度科学数据探索）这一智能体任务范式，并开发了SciDataSailor框架，通过蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）合成工具交互轨迹，使LLM智能体能够在真实科学数据仓库中进行导航、文件解析、分析执行与跨文件证据整合，最终构建了SciDataSailor-SFT-2K训练集和SciDataSailor-Bench评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据集通常以层级仓库形式组织，包含异构且相互依赖的文件，人工检查和集成分析工作繁重且依赖领域专家。尽管LLM智能体在规划、推理和工具使用方面已有显著进展，但现有研究大多忽略了它们通过可执行环境与真实科学数据资产交互的能力。

Method: 提出了SciDataSailor框架，将轨迹合成建模为蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），并引入四个任务特化机制：难度分层探索种子、双反馈首次落子紧迫性、层级策略到工具动作生成、以及熵引导分支。基于该框架构建了用于监督微调的SciDataSailor-SFT-2K和用于评测的SciDataSailor-Bench。

Result: 构建的SciDataSailor-Bench包含627个元信息摘要任务和586个科学问答任务，覆盖生命科学、地球科学和物理科学领域的27个数据集，用于系统评估智能体的科学数据探索能力。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统性地将LLM智能体引入真实科学数据资产的可执行环境交互场景，提出了一种平衡广泛探索与定向利用的轨迹合成方法，为科学数据智能探索领域提供了新的任务范式、训练框架和评测基准。

Abstract: Scientific datasets are commonly organized as hierarchical repositories containing heterogeneous and interdependent files, making their inspection, integration, and analysis labor-intensive and reliant on domain expertise. Although large language model (LLM) agents have advanced substantially in planning, reasoning, and tool use, existing research has largely overlooked their ability to interact with real scientific data assets through executable environments. We introduce Deep Scientific Data Exploration, an agentic task paradigm in which agents navigate repositories, interpret heterogeneous files and schemas, execute analyses, integrate cross-file evidence, and produce conclusions grounded in executed observations. To operationalize this paradigm, we present SciDataSailor, a framework for synthesizing tool-interactive trajectories by balancing broad exploration with targeted exploitation. SciDataSailor instantiates trajectory synthesis as Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) with four task-specific mechanisms: difficulty-stratified exploration seeds, dual-feedback first-play urgency, hierarchical strategy-to-tool action generation, and entropy-guided branching. Using this framework, we construct SciDataSailor-SFT-2K for supervised fine-tuning and SciDataSailor-Bench for evaluation, with the latter comprising 627 meta-information summarization tasks and 586 scientific question-answering tasks across 27 datasets spanning the life, earth, and physical sciences.

</details>


### [252] [IndustryForge-27B: A Domain-Enhanced Multimodal Foundation Model for Industrial CAD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28050)
*Nianchen Deng,Jiaxin Ai,Tao Hu,Shu Zou,Yurui Dong,Siqi Li,Xinyu Cai,Xuemeng Yang,Licheng Wen,Hongbin Zhou,Hairong Zhang,Pinlong Cai,Botian Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IndustryForge-27B，一个基于Qwen3.5-VL-27B构建的工业CAD多模态基础模型。通过整合六个工业CAD子语料库（约52k多模态样本），使用统一的多任务SFT配方训练，模型在四个CAD基准上平均提升基础模型33.65个百分点，并全面超越GPT-5.4；在十一个通用能力基准上保持甚至小幅提升（+1.56pp），无灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业CAD设计与制造对多模态基础模型提出了独特且多样化的需求：模型需要理解工程图纸和3D几何截图，编写参数化建模脚本和Windows COM API代码，并覆盖从单个零件到装配体的全范围。通用多模态模型在这些任务上表现不足，而单一任务的微调又过于狭窄，难以支持上层智能体发出的多样化调用需求。

Method: 在Qwen3.5-VL-27B基础上，策划并整合六个工业CAD子语料库（总计约52k多模态样本）：CAD视觉问答（CAD-VQA）、参数化CAD代码（text2cadquery）、装配级CAD代码（text2cadquery-assembly），以及面向Inventor/SolidWorks的三个COM子语料库（com_2d/com_3d/com_assembly），并使用统一的多任务SFT（监督微调）配方进行训练。

Result: 在四个CAD领域基准上，IndustryForge-27B平均比基础模型提升33.65个百分点，并在全部四个基准上超越强大的闭源模型GPT-5.4；在十一个通用能力基准上，模型保持并小幅提升了基础模型的表现（平均+1.56pp），没有出现灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: IndustryForge-27B将作为下游工业智能体项目的公共基础，为全栈式工业智能体提供统一的起点，覆盖从CAD设计到工业软件操作、从零件到装配体、从单次生成到闭环自我改进的完整范围。

Abstract: Automating industrial CAD design and manufacturing places distinctive demands on multimodal foundation models: the model must see engineering drawings and 3D geometry screenshots, write correct parametric-modelling scripts and Windows COM API code, and cover the full range from single parts to assemblies. General-purpose multimodal models fall short on these tasks, while single-task fine-tuning is too narrow to support the diverse calls that upper-layer agents issue. We build IndustryForge-27B on top of Qwen3.5-VL-27B by curating and integrating six industrial-CAD sub-corpora totalling $\sim$52k multimodal samples---CAD Visual QA (CAD-VQA), parametric CAD code (text2cadquery), assembly-level CAD code (text2cadquery-assembly), and three COM sub-corpora for Inventor / SolidWorks (com_2d / com_3d / com_assembly)---and training with a unified multi-task SFT recipe. Across four CAD-domain benchmarks IndustryForge-27B lifts the base model by $+33.65$~pp on average and outperforms the strong closed-source model GPT-5.4 on all four; across eleven general-capability benchmarks it retains, and slightly improves upon, the base model ($+1.56$~pp mean, no catastrophic forgetting). IndustryForge-27B will serve as the common substrate for downstream industrial-agent projects, providing a unified starting point for a full-stack industrial agent that spans from CAD design to industrial-software operation, from parts to assemblies, and from single-shot generation to closed-loop self-improvement.

</details>


### [253] [CDAE: Enhancing Perturbation Robustness in Pretrained Language Models with Contrastive Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28236)
*Sina Heydari,Amirreza Abbasi,Mohsen Hooshmand,Majid Ramezani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级对比去噪自编码器（CDAE），通过联合优化对比学习和重建目标，对预训练BERT生成的句子嵌入进行精炼，使其对同义词替换、掩码和词丢弃等语义保持性扰动具有鲁棒性（即扰动不变性），实验结果表明其嵌入相似性保持优于原始BERT和SimCSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语言模型显著提升了句子表示学习，但其嵌入对语义保持性文本扰动（如同义词替换、掩码和词丢弃）仍然敏感，导致表示不稳定，影响下游任务的可靠性。

Method: 提出轻量级对比去噪自编码器（CDAE），在预训练BERT嵌入基础上，联合优化对比学习目标（增强扰动不变性）和重建目标（保留语义信息），以学习扰动不变的句子表示。评估中采用多种扰动策略及不同强度，并与原始BERT嵌入和SimCSE进行对比。

Result: 实验结果表明，CDAE在各种扰动策略下始终能保持更高的嵌入相似性，且随着扰动强度的增加，其优势更加明显，说明该方法有效增强了表示稳定性同时保留了语义信息。

Conclusion: 扰动不变学习是改进句子嵌入的一个有前景的方向，CDAE在保持语义的同时增强了表示的稳定性，代码已公开。

Abstract: Pre-trained language models have significantly improved sentence representation learning, yet their embedding remain sensitive to semantic preserving textual perturbations such as synonym substitution, masking and word dropout. This work proposes a lightweight Contrastive Denoising Autoencoder (CDAE) that refines pre-trained BERT embedding by jointly optimizing contrastive and reconstruction objective to learn perturbation-invariant representation. We evaluate the proposed framework using multiple perturbation strategies with varying strengths and compare it against the original BERT embeddings and SimCSE. Experimental results show that CDAE consistently preserves higher embedding similarity under perturbations, with the improvements becoming more pronounced as framework effectively enhances representation stability while preserving semantic information, highlighting perturbation-invariant learning as a promising direction for improving sentence embeddings. The source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/ComputationIASBS/CDAE

</details>


### [254] [SemPIC: Learning Semantic Position-Independent KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28069)
*Hui Xie,Peng Xiao,Yutong Deng\textsuperscript,Shuoran Dou,Jian Yang,Jinyang Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemPIC是一种通过学习边界条件化文档KV表示的方法，通过行为蒸馏训练一个LoRA Writer来离线编译文档KV缓存，保留预训练解码器作为不变的Reader，在长上下文检索和智能体工作负载中显著提升缓存命中时的性能，平均micro-F1从0.53提升至0.60，接近全重计算的0.62。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文检索和智能体工作负载会反复复用相同文档，但指令、历史和文档顺序会变化。前缀缓存无法利用这种复用模式，而位置无关缓存（PIC）因独立编译的KV状态缺乏未来消费上下文而不可靠。诊断发现学习的边界条件化基线能显著降低可复用块边界附近的注意力偏差，但内部和任务级残差仍然存在，因此需要适应文档表示本身。

Method: SemPIC训练一个启用LoRA的Writer，通过行为蒸馏将逐层文档KV编译为原生表示，同时保留预训练解码器作为不变的Reader。适应性仅限制在离线缓存构建阶段，保持标准KV接口和缓存命中解码路径。此外引入KV梯度检查点技术，在不切断KV缓存梯度的情况下减少峰值训练内存。

Result: 在三个模型和四个任务上，SemPIC将平均micro-F1从KV Packet的0.53提升至0.60，接近Full Recompute的0.62。

Conclusion: SemPIC通过将适应限制在离线缓存构建阶段，在不改变标准KV接口和推理路径的前提下，大幅缩小了PIC与全重计算之间的性能差距，为长上下文复用场景提供了高效且可靠的缓存方案。

Abstract: Long-context retrieval and agentic workloads repeatedly reuse the same documents under changing instructions, histories, and document orders. Prefix caching cannot exploit this reuse, while position-independent caching (PIC) remains unreliable because independently compiled KV states lack the future context in which they will be consumed. Our diagnostics show that a learned boundary-conditioned baseline sharply reduces attention deviation near reusable-block boundaries but leaves interior and task-level residuals, motivating adaptation of the document representation itself. We present \emph{SemPIC}, which trains a LoRA-enabled Writer to compile native per-layer document KVs through behavioral distillation while retaining the pretrained decoder as an unchanged Reader. Adaptation is confined to offline cache construction, preserving the standard KV interface and cache-hit decoding path. We further introduce KV Gradient Checkpointing, which reduces peak training memory without severing gradients through cached KVs. Across three models and four tasks, SemPIC raises mean micro-F1 over KV Packet from 0.53 to 0.60, approaching Full Recompute at 0.62.

</details>


### [255] [GLM-RAG: Graph Language Models for Graph-Based Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28397)
*Maya Arseven,Anette Frank,Beni Egressy,Johann Higl,Moritz Plenz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入了一种基于图语言模型（GLM）的检索器，系统比较了GLM、GNN和传统向量检索三类检索器在单跳和多跳RAG任务中的表现，重点考察跨领域泛化能力。实验表明，微调后的GLM检索器在跨域多跳基准上达到SOTA并展现出更好的泛化性；GNN检索器在训练效率和图覆盖上占优；向量检索基准则在单跳数据集上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱上的检索增强生成（RAG）需要检索器同时捕捉图结构和语义信息。已有工作分别探索了基于GNN的检索器以及图语言模型（GLM），但缺乏对这两类方法以及传统向量检索方法在单跳/多跳RAG场景下系统性的比较研究，尤其是在跨域可迁移性方面。

Method: 提出一种基于GLM的检索器，并将其与GNN检索器、传统向量搜索检索器在单跳和多跳RAG设定下进行对比实验；同时考察参数规模与子图覆盖对性能的影响，以及各方法在未见领域上的迁移能力。

Result: 微调后的GLM检索器在域外泛化上优于其他方法，在两项多跳基准上达到SOTA；在域内多跳问答数据集上与现有工作相当，且随参数规模和子图覆盖增加展现出良好的扩展性。GNN检索器在高效训练下达到较高图覆盖，向量搜索基线则在单跳数据集上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 不同类型的检索器在不同任务设定下各有优势：GLM检索器适合跨域多跳推理且泛化性强，GNN检索器在训练效率和图覆盖间取得了良好平衡，向量检索仍适用于单跳场景。研究为知识图谱RAG系统的检索器选型提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over knowledge graphs requires retrievers that can effectively capture both graph structure and semantic information. Recent approaches have explored graph neural network (GNN)-based retrievers to model graph topology in multi-hop reasoning tasks. In parallel, graph language models (GLMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm that integrates graph reasoning and the semantic capabilities of language models. In this work, we introduce a GLM-based retriever and investigate the comparative strengths of GLM-based, GNN-based, and traditional vector-search-based retrievers in single- and multi-hop RAG settings, and with a particular focus on transferability to unseen domains. Our findings suggest that finetuned GLM retrievers generalize better out of domain, achieving SOTA on two multi-hop benchmarks. On in-domain multi-hop QA datasets they remain comparable to prior work, with promising scaling as parameters and subgraph coverage increase. GNN-based retrievers achieve higher graph coverage with an efficient training setup, whereas the vector-search baseline excels at single-hop datasets.

</details>


### [256] [Echoverse: Deep, Evolving Environments for Training Computer-Use Agents at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28074)
*Yash Pandya,Sahil Gupta,Sarthak Harne,Archana Yadav,Kavyansh Chourasia,Hussein Mozannar,Vibhav Vineet,Sara Abdali,Corby Rosset,Yash Lara,Ahmed Awadallah,Ece Kamar,Akshay Nambi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Echoverse，一种生成有状态计算机使用（computer-use）训练环境的编译框架，通过将规格说明编译为可交互应用，并利用"共同进化"循环——将每次分级回放同时用于环境/任务/验证器的修复和模型的训练信号，从而提升智能体在真实网站上的表现。在12个环境上训练的9B模型从36.5%提升至67.1%，逼近其教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体需要大量真实且可重置的应用环境进行训练，但最关键的登录门控且有状态的应用难以规模化获取。虽然近期流水线可批量生成合成环境，但瓶颈已从环境数量转向环境质量——需要明确哪些属性决定了环境对训练的实际价值。

Method: 1) 提出Echoverse：将规格说明编译为有状态应用，任务基于应用自身数据库进行分级校验；2) 提出共同进化（co-evolution）循环：每次分级回放被双重利用——作为环境/任务/验证器的修复输入，也作为模型训练信号；3) 通过消融实验验证三个关键属性：行为深度（behavioural depth）、目标化交互（targeting specific failures）和与环境共同改进（improvement alongside the model）；4) 同一环境也用作强化学习环境，使用接地验证器与稠密逐步评判器的组合奖励。

Result: 在12个环境上训练的9B模型在14个评估子集上从36.5%提升至67.1%，领先教师模型（更大的前沿模型）仅14个百分点。消融实验显示：浅层环境使在线准确率从80.0降至75.0，深层环境则提升至85.0和65.0；针对单一界面控件跨多种渲染的训练可迁移至未见过的控件族及开放网页；修复单个环境将训练模型从16.2%提升至38.5%。在强化学习设置下，结合接地验证器与稠密逐步评判器的奖励将保留测试分数从58.8%提升至68.0%。

Conclusion: 合成训练环境的价值取决于三个属性：行为深度、对智能体真实失败点的目标化、以及随模型能力共同进化的能力。Echoverse验证了通过"环境即编译+共同进化"范式可有效提升计算机使用智能体的泛化能力，并开源了四个环境基准。

Abstract: Computer-use agents learn from what their actions change, so training one needs applications it can act on, break and reset. The applications that matter most are login-gated and stateful, so synthetic environments stand in for them. Recent pipelines generate such environments in bulk, which moves the bottleneck from how many exist to what is inside each one. The returns, we find, come from three properties: how much behavioural depth an environment carries, whether it targets the interaction an agent actually fails, and whether it improves alongside the model. We present Echoverse, which compiles specifications into stateful applications whose tasks are graded against the application's own database, and a co-evolution loop that reads every graded rollout twice: as repairs to the environment, its tasks and its verifier, and as training signal for the model. Trained on twelve such environments, a 9B model improves from $36.5\%$ to $67.1\%$ across fourteen evaluation splits, within fourteen points of the much larger frontier model that taught it. We examine each property in turn. On the same domains, shallow environments push live-site accuracy below the base model ($80.0 \to 75.0$) while deep ones raise it ($80.0 \to 85.0$ and $48.0 \to 65.0$); drilling one interface control across many renderings transfers to held-out widget families and to the open web; and repairing a single environment lifts the model trained on it from $16.2\%$ to $38.5\%$. The same worlds serve as reinforcement-learning environments, where a reward combining the grounded verifier with a dense per-step judge raises held-out score from $58.8\%$ to $68.0\%$. We release four environments as a benchmark, with their applications, seed data and grounded graders. Code: https://aka.ms/echoverse

</details>


### [257] [WIDE: Boosting Adaptive LLM Inference via Token-level Dynamic Width Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28418)
*Haozhe Hu,Hao Wu,Peiran Yin,Chao Han,Yunpu Ma,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WIDE是首个端到端可微分的Token级动态宽度剪枝框架，支持预填充（prefill）和解码（decode）场景，通过在神经元块级别（注意力头组和FFN通道组）进行细粒度动态计算分配，结合两阶段训练和剪枝-内核协同设计，在50%稀疏度下比现有动态深度剪枝方法提升55.1%性能，并实现最高4.95倍的理论内核级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态结构化剪枝方法硬件友好但输入无关的计算分配导致精度下降；近期动态剪枝方法虽能根据输入适应计算，但多限于粗粒度的层级结构决策，且实际推理场景中的加速效果具有挑战性。

Method: 1) 将动态剪枝从层级扩展至神经元块级别（注意力头组和FFN通道组），实现Token级别的细粒度计算分配；2) 采用两阶段训练流程学习Token级稀疏执行模式；3) 提出剪枝-内核协同设计框架，将动态稀疏加速分解为掩码重排序、硬件无关的块级跳过程、硬件相关的块内跳过程三个组件，实现跨不同粒度的高效执行。

Result: 在50%稀疏度下，与最先进的动态深度剪枝方法相比提升55.1%的性能（校准设置下）；在预填充和解码推理工作负载中，实现最高1.98倍（预填充）和4.95倍（解码）的接近理论内核级加速，以及1.68倍和1.55倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: WIDE证明了通过Token级动态宽度剪枝结合剪枝-内核协同设计，可以在保持较高精度保留的同时实现实际推理场景中的高效加速，为动态剪枝在LLM高效推理中的落地提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Pruning is a promising approach for improving the efficiency of LLMs. Existing static structured pruning methods are hardware-friendly and can deliver practical throughput gains, but their input-agnostic computation allocation often causes substantial accuracy degradation under aggressive sparsity. Recent dynamic sparsity methods improve quality retention by adapting computation to individual inputs, yet they remain largely limited to coarse-grained structural decisions and their practical acceleration under real-world inference scenarios remains challenging. To address these challenges, we present WIDE, the first end-to-end differentiable token-level dynamic width pruning framework designed for both prefill and decode scenarios. WIDE enables fine-grained computation allocation by allowing each token to dynamically select attention-head groups and FFN-channel groups, extending dynamic pruning beyond layer-level decisions to neuron-block-level granularity. Through a two-stage training pipeline, WIDE learns effective token-wise sparse execution patterns and achieves substantially better quality retention than existing approaches. To make such fine-grained dynamic pruning practical, we further propose a pruning--kernel co-design framework that decomposes dynamic sparsity acceleration into mask reordering, hardware-agnostic block-level skipping, and hardware-dependent intra-block skipping, enabling efficient execution across different granularities. At 50% sparsity, WIDE provides 55.1% performance boost when compared to the state-of-the-art dynamic depth pruning under calibration-only settings. Under prefill and decoding inference workloads, WIDE achieves close-to-theoretical kernel-level speedups of up to 1.98x for prefill and 4.95x for decoding, as well as 1.68x and 1.55x end-to-end acceleration. Our code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/LLM-Pruning/tree/main/WIDE.

</details>


### [258] [Group-Reflective Self-Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28076)
*Binbin Zheng,Zijun Xie,Guanqun Zhao,Enlei Gong,Xing Ma,Xiaoliang Fu,Zeyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Group-Reflective Self-Distillation (GRSD)方法，通过让策略模型对自身验证过的rollout进行分组反思，并利用停止梯度快照对比成功与失败轨迹的反思结果来构建分组特权指导，从而实现转向级别信用分配，在多个智能体环境中优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中的终端奖励仅提供粗略的轨迹级监督，成功行为、重复错误和偶然选择被纠缠在同一结果信号中。现有的智能体自蒸馏方法虽然能用自然语言技能丰富稀疏监督，但外部检索或由更强模型从单一轨迹提取的技能可能与当前经验不匹配、超出策略能力或过于路径特定。

Method: GRSD方法包括三个关键组件：(1) 针对每个提示，策略模型对on-policy组中的每个已验证轨迹进行反思；(2) 使用停止梯度快照对比成功与失败rollout的反思结果，构建分组级特权指导；(3) 基于该指导，self-teacher通过调节基于结果的advantage来实现转向级信用分配，同时保持验证器决定的学习方向。

Result: 在多个智能体环境和不同模型规模上的实验表明，GRSD在多个基准上持续优于竞争性基线，并且在未见任务上具有更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: GRSD通过从策略自身已验证rollout中提取能力匹配且具有结果判别性的指导，有效解决了RLVR中终端奖励监督粒度粗和外部技能不匹配的问题，提升了智能体训练的有效性和泛化性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is effective for training large language model agents. However, terminal rewards provide only coarse trajectory-level supervision, leaving successful behaviors, recurring mistakes, and incidental choices entangled in the same outcome signal. Existing agentic self-distillation methods enrich sparse supervision with natural-language skills, but skills retrieved externally or extracted from a single trajectory by stronger models may mismatch current experience, exceed the policy's capability, or remain path-specific. We propose Group-Reflective Self-Distillation (GRSD), which derives capability-aligned and outcome-discriminative guidance from the policy's own verified rollouts. For each prompt, the policy reflects on each verified trajectory in an on-policy group, and a stop-gradient snapshot contrasts the resulting reflections from successful and failed rollouts to construct group-level privileged guidance. Conditioned on this guidance, a self-teacher refines turn-level credit assignment by modulating outcome-based advantages while preserving the verifier-determined learning direction. Experiments across multiple agentic environments and model scales demonstrate that GRSD consistently outperforms competitive baselines and generalizes more effectively to unseen tasks.

</details>


### [259] [Metaphor Tracer: A Theory-Informed Analysis of Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28434)
*Marc Heimann,Roxana Assadi Moghaddam,Olga Brovkina,Mark Pettifor,Lutz Goetzmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于隐藏状态的无监督评分方法——聚合器（aggregator）与分化器（differentiator），分别衡量一个token位置是否整合整个文本、以及是否将其他token暂时"运输"进其子空间。聚合器不依赖训练，且区别于经典信息量或显著性度量；在三款不同模型中，重复符号的surprisal与注意力下降而其聚合分数保持不变，表明该通道标记token在文本中的位置。独立真值验证（6/6与34/36单元格）支持其跟踪阅读过程，迁移测试进一步表明该度量表征token在特定文本中的关系性位置，而非其向量本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型隐藏状态能否捕捉单一文本的组织结构，验证一种关系性（而非本质主义）的隐藏状态解读，并操作化先于工具存在的理论。

Method: 作者从单次前向传播、无需训练地对每个token位置计算两个分数：聚合器衡量该位置将全文整合为稳定配置的程度；分化器衡量其他token在模型阅读过程中被瞬时携入其子空间的程度（即"运输"）。通过冻结超参数于一篇发现文本，在其余文本上进行同分布与跨模型确认、独立真值对齐分析及迁移测试。

Result: 跨三个不相关模型，重复符号的surprisal与注意下降但聚合分数保持稳定；聚合器在工程化语域中跨边界跟随（6/6单元格），并在精神分析师预先标注的临床转录中覆盖34/36单元格，呈分级递增且与词汇控制分离，无类型级度量可复现。迁移测试显示：token结构随词汇类型流转的模型对单篇话语阅读最差；在匹配的基础/指令微调对中，微调提升保真度而不移动类型迁移。

Conclusion: 结构价值是token在"这篇"文本中位置的属性，而非其向量的固有属性；这支持对隐藏状态的关系性而非本质主义解读，并为预存理论提供了操作性验证。

Abstract: What do a language model's hidden states say about the organization of a single text? From one forward pass, without training, we score every token position on two properties. The *aggregator* measures whether the position consolidates the whole text into a stable configuration. The *differentiator*, whether other tokens are transiently carried into its subspace as the model reads: metaphor in its root sense, transport. Constants were frozen on one discovery text; every other is confirmatory.
  The aggregator is not, in the classic sense, an information measure, nor a measure of salience. Across three unrelated models, as a signifier repeats, its surprisal and its attention drain while its aggregator score holds: the channel marks a token's place in the text. That this tracks a reading rests on independent ground truth: an engineered register the aggregator follows across its boundaries (6/6 cells), and a psychoanalyst's marking of clinical transcripts, fixed before the instrument existed, in 34/36 cells, with a graded increment above lexical controls and dissociations no type-level measure reproduces. A transfer test gives the result its shape: the model whose token structure travels with lexical type reads the singular discourse worst, and in a matched base/instruct pair tuning raises fidelity without moving type-transfer. Structural value is a property of a token's place in *this* text, not of its vector alone: a relational rather than essentialist reading of hidden states, operationalizing theory that predated the instrument.

</details>


### [260] [Distilling Answer Set Programming Theories from Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28086)
*Nelson Higuera Ruiz,Markus Hofmarcher,Claudiu Leoveanu-Condrei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究神经符号方法下，模型能否在给定代理框架和求解器回路的条件下，从零开始蒸馏出完整正确的ASP理论。在三个VQA基准（CLEVR、GQA、CLEVRER）上测试了九个模型（四个前沿模型、两个中端模型、三个开源模型），前沿模型中三个在CLEVR上达到100%，在GQA和CLEVRER上分别达到92.8%-98.8%和92.7%-95.3%；GPT-5表现参差不齐。加入参考理论对其他模型影响不超过±3.4个百分点，但使GPT-5准确率下降3-19个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从零编写ASP理论是困难且耗时的任务，作者希望探索是否可以通过神经符号方法让模型自动蒸馏出完整且正确的ASP理论，以降低人工编写逻辑程序的门槛。

Method: 采用数据集无关的协议：给定固定的代理框架，以单个提示词和空文件作为起点，模型在1小时时间限制内从零推导完整理论。选择VQA领域（CLEVR、GQA、CLEVRER三个基准），并用九个不同规模的模型（前沿：Claude Sonnet 4.6、Claude Opus 4.7、GPT-5、DeepSeek V4 Pro；中端：DeepSeek V4 Flash、gpt-oss-120b；开源：qwen3.6-27b、gpt-oss-20b、qwen3.5-9b）研究模型规模与任务求解能力的关系。

Result: 四个前沿模型中有三个在CLEVR上达到100%，在GQA上达到92.8%-98.8%，在CLEVRER上达到92.7%-95.3%。GPT-5在CLEVR上达到98.7%，但在GQA上骤降至41.8%，在CLEVRER上降至86.7%。添加其他数据集的参考理论对其他三个前沿模型影响不超过±3.4个百分点，但使GPT-5的准确率下降3-19个百分点。

Conclusion: 前沿模型具备从零蒸馏完整ASP理论的能力，但不同模型间表现差异显著，且外部参考理论对不同模型的影响不一致。作者开源了代码、提示词和蒸馏出的理论。

Abstract: Writing Answer Set Programming (ASP) theories from scratch is a difficult and time-consuming task. We take a neurosymbolic approach to study whether a model can distill complete and correct theories, given a fixed agent harness with the solver in the loop. The protocol is dataset-agnostic: with a single prompt and an empty file as the starting point the model is given a 1-hour time limit to derive a complete theory. We chose VQA as the application domain, three benchmarks (CLEVR, GQA, CLEVRER), as these are publicly available and non-trivial. In order to study the model scale required for solving this task we nine different models: four frontier (Claude Sonnet 4.6, Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5, DeepSeek V4 Pro), two mid-tier (DeepSeek V4 Flash, gpt-oss-120b), and three open-weights (qwen3.6-27b, gpt-oss-20b, qwen3.5-9b). Three of four frontier models reach 100% on CLEVR and 92.8%-98.8% on GQA; on CLEVRER, Sonnet, Opus, DeepSeek V4 Pro score 92.7%-95.3%. GPT-5 reaches 98.7% on CLEVR but drops to 41.8% on GQA and to 86.7% on CLEVRER. Adding handwritten reference theories from other datasets moves the other three frontier models by at most +/-3.4 pp but reduces GPT-5's accuracy by 3-19 pp. We release the code, prompts, and theories distilled.

</details>


### [261] [SVR: Self-Verifying Refinement via Joint Verdict-Confidence Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28457)
*Hongyu Chen,Liang Lin,Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SVR框架，一种无外部奖励模型的自我验证强化学习方法，通过GRPO训练让模型在每轮生成解、正确性判定和置信度分数，据此自适应决定是否停止推理或继续改进，在7个数学推理基准上以平均2.99轮推理达到0.563的宏平均准确率，优于固定预算和其他多轮基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时计算扩展方法存在两个问题：统一预算在简单输入上浪费计算资源，而依赖验证器引导的细化方法需要外部反馈信号，增加了部署复杂性和额外成本。作者希望设计一种无需外部Oracle反馈、模型自身即可进行自适应计算分配的内部控制机制。

Method: SVR采用无Oracle的多轮强化学习框架。模型在每轮生成解的同时输出离散正确性判定和置信度分数；仅当判定为Correct且置信度超过阈值时保留当前答案，否则利用自身自我验证继续细化。训练阶段使用GRPO在固定水平轨迹上优化，奖励函数同时促进解正确性、校准感知的自我验证以及可停止的正确状态；自适应停止仅在推理阶段激活。ground-truth正确性仅用于构造训练奖励，推理时完全不需要。

Result: 在7个数学推理基准上用Qwen3.5-2B评估，SVR达到0.563的宏平均准确率，仅需平均2.99轮推理。在完整系统比较中，超越标准GRPO、强多轮基线和固定预算的Oracle引导评分反馈参考方法，同时比固定10轮推理使用显著更少的推理轮数。

Conclusion: 学习到的自我验证可以作为有效的内部控制信号，用于答案保留决策和自适应测试时计算分配，在显著节省推理成本的同时保持甚至提升推理准确率，验证了无外部验证器的自适应计算缩放路线的可行性。

Abstract: Scaling test-time computation can improve language-model reasoning, but uniform budgets waste computation on easy inputs, while verifier-guided refinement relies on external feedback. We introduce Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR), an oracle-free multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that learns to use self-verification as a compute-control policy. At each turn, the model produces a solution together with a discrete correctness verdict and a confidence score; it retains the current answer only when the verdict is Correct and confidence exceeds a threshold, and otherwise continues refinement using its own self-verification. Ground-truth correctness is used only to construct training rewards and is never exposed to the policy through refinement prompts or required at inference. SVR is trained with GRPO on fixed-horizon trajectories using rewards that promote solution correctness, calibration-aware self-verification, and stop-ready correct states; adaptive stopping is activated only at inference. On seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3.5-2B, SVR achieves a macro-average accuracy of 0.563 with only 2.99 inference turns on average. In the evaluated complete-system comparison, it exceeds standard GRPO, strong multi-turn baselines, and a fixed-budget oracle-guided score-feedback reference while requiring substantially fewer turns than fixed ten-turn inference. These results demonstrate that learned self-verification can serve as an effective internal control signal for answer retention and adaptive test-time compute allocation.

</details>


### [262] [Diversifying Personalized Research Ideation against AI-Induced Homogenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28087)
*Rui Xu,Yunke Wang,Linwei Tao,Wenjie Xuan,Yong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DivAlign，一个四阶段流水线，用于生成既保持研究者个性化对齐又降低社区级冗余的研究方向建议。通过在95名AI研究者的基准上进行验证，DivAlign在保持研究者-方向匹配度的同时，显著降低了社区层面的冗余（平均成对相似度从0.331降至0.294，最近邻相似度从0.704降至0.608），并保留了99.9%的方向匹配得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助研究构思系统存在两个盲点：一是粗略的研究者表征可能引出缺乏研究者特定根基的主流方向；二是独立推荐会导致社区研究组合集中在反复出现的高概率主题上，造成同质化。这两个问题源于当前系统孤立地优化单一建议，缺乏对研究者细粒度画像和社区组合层面的全局考量。

Method: DivAlign是一个四阶段流水线：(1) 提取细粒度的研究者画像；(2) 生成以画像为条件的候选研究方向；(3) 从三个对齐维度（可执行性、可理解性和增长潜力）对候选方向进行评分；(4) 在减少社区组合冗余的同时，呈现研究者本地的方向建议。

Result: 在由95名AI研究者（覆盖五个子领域）构建的基准上，与粗粒度单次构思相比，DivAlign将平均成对相似度从0.331降至0.294，最近邻相似度从0.704降至0.608；与独立的top-choice变体相比，最近邻相似度从0.663降至0.608，同时保留了99.9%的研究者-方向匹配得分。

Conclusion: DivAlign通过细粒度画像建模与社区级去冗余的联合优化，有效解决了AI辅助研究构思中的个性化缺失与社区同质化两个盲点，在保持研究者方向契合度的前提下实现了研究组合的多样性提升，为研究构思系统提供了一个有前景的范式。

Abstract: AI-assisted research ideation has emerged as a promising paradigm for accelerating scientific discovery, with systems now capable of generating research directions conditioned on papers, topics, or lightweight researcher contexts. Yet current systems largely optimize individual suggestions in isolation. This leaves two blind spots. First, coarse researcher representations may elicit mainstream directions that appear broadly feasible, but lack sufficient researcher-specific grounding. Second, independent recommendations can concentrate a community's portfolio around recurring high-probability themes. To address these blind spots, we propose DivAlign, a four-stage pipeline for alignment-preserving de-homogenization. DivAlign extracts fine-grained researcher profiles, generates profile-conditioned candidate directions, scores them along three alignment dimensions (Executability, Comprehensibility, and Growth Potential), and surfaces researcher-local directions while reducing redundancy across the community portfolio. On a benchmark we construct from 95 AI researchers across five subfields, DivAlign reduces community-level redundancy while preserving researcher-direction fit. Compared with coarse single-shot ideation, it lowers average pairwise similarity from 0.331 to 0.294 and nearest-neighbor similarity from 0.704 to 0.608. Compared with the independent top-choice variant, DivAlign reduces nearest-neighbor similarity from 0.663 to 0.608 while retaining 99.9% of the researcher-direction fit score. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Ruixxxx/DivAlign.

</details>


### [263] [PerturbMap: Cross-Context Transfer of Single-Cell Perturbation Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28090)
*Panpan Cui,Yiqi Liu,Wenhao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PerturbMap框架，通过将同一扰动在源上下文中已测得的响应，经源到接收者的岭回归专家迁移，与接收者局部低秩基底结合，预测缺失的接收者上下文中的扰动效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞扰动图谱很少覆盖所有干预在所有细胞上下文中的组合，导致查询扰动在目标上下文中的效应缺失。忽略已有测量会浪费查询特异的实验证据，而直接复制或弱校准跨上下文信号则有错误迁移的风险。

Method: PerturbMap结合接收者局部低秩基底与可接受的候选方案——即将同一扰动在源上下文的测量响应，通过基于配对训练扰动拟合的源到接收者岭回归专家进行迁移，候选权重由在验证锚点上估计的路径可靠性决定。

Result: 在Perturb-CITE-seq黑色素瘤队列上，PerturbMap相比接收者局部低秩基底将全效应MSE降低4.1%，并优于FedAvg、零响应、原始复制、校准复制和身份混洗仿射等对照方法；与使用更强训练接口的集中式token匹配池化参考相比，MSE仅相差2.82×10⁻⁶。同接收者top-10余弦检索率从低秩基底的74.5%提升至PerturbMap的80.5%。

Conclusion: PerturbMap通过路径可靠性加权的跨上下文迁移，有效利用源上下文中的查询特异实验证据，在保持接近集中式参照性能的同时，显著优于各类基线，验证了其在不完整单细胞扰动图谱中进行跨上下文效应预测的有效性。

Abstract: Single-cell perturbation atlases rarely measure every intervention in every cellular context: a query perturbation is often observed in one or more source contexts but missing in the recipient context where its effect is needed. Ignoring those measured responses discards query-specific experimental evidence, whereas copying or weakly calibrating them across contexts risks transferring the wrong signal. We propose PerturbMap, which predicts a missing recipient-context effect by combining a recipient-local low-rank base with accepted proposals that transport the same perturbation's measured source responses through source-to-recipient ridge experts fit on paired training perturbations, with proposal weights determined by route reliability estimated on validation anchors. On the Perturb-CITE-seq melanoma cohort, PerturbMap improves full-effect MSE by 4.1\% over a recipient-local low-rank base and achieves lower MSE than FedAvg, zero-response, raw-copy, calibrated-copy, and identity-shuffled affine controls. It remains within $2.82\times10^{-6}$ MSE of our centralized token-matched pooled reference, which uses a stronger training interface. A condition-mean specificity diagnostic shows the same direction: same-recipient top-10 counterpart retrieval by cosine increases from 74.5\% for the low-rank base to 80.5\% for PerturbMap.

</details>


### [264] [OSReward: Instituting Standardized Evaluation for Cross-Platform Computer-Use Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28609)
*Qiushi Sun,Kanzhi Cheng,Yian Wang,Bowen Yang,Hang Yan,Liheng Chen,Fangzhi Xu,Zichen Ding,Nuo Chen,Jialin Cao,Xingdong Gong,Zehao Li,Kaiming Jin,Xinfeng Yuan,Zhoumianze Liu,Jingyang Gong,Zhangyue Yin,Jiahui Gao,Zhiyong Wu,Tianbao Xie,Jianbing Zhang,Ben Kao,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了OSReward基准测试来系统评估VLM评委在计算机使用代理（CUA）轨迹验证中的可靠性，发现最先进的VLM评委存在系统性宽容偏差且成本过高，因此构建了OS-Shepherd-100K语料库并训练了开源奖励模型OS-Shepherd（9B和35B），以30-60%的成本匹配商业评委的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUA轨迹验证对于代理评估、数据整理和强化学习至关重要，但人工验证无法规模化，因此领域越来越依赖VLM作为评委。然而，这些VLM评委的可靠性从未被系统研究过，这一问题亟需解决。

Method: 构建OSReward基准：从多种代理主干平台收集轨迹，经多阶段人工标注获得可靠的真实判定；从中提取OSReward-Hard（困难案例）和OSReward-Multi（细粒度效率与对齐评分）。此外构建OS-Shepherd-100K开放语料库，包含带推理标注的轨迹判定，并在此之上训练OS-Shepherd（9B和35B）开源奖励模型。

Result: 对VLM评委最全面的评估发现：最先进模型仍达不到理想评委标准，存在系统性宽容偏差（将失败运行误标为成功）；少数可靠的评委成本过高无法规模化部署，开源模型性能落后较大。OS-Shepherd以30-60%的成本达到商业评委的性能水平。

Conclusion: VLM评委在CUA轨迹验证中存在显著的可靠性缺口，尤其是宽容偏差和成本问题。开放奖励模型OS-Shepherd提供了低成本、稳定且可靠的替代方案，能缩小与商业模型的差距，为大规模CUA奖励信号设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Computer-using agents (CUAs) are advancing rapidly across the digital world. A CUA trajectory records the agent's actions, states, and reasoning. Verifying whether it fulfilled the task instruction is central to CUA evaluation, data curation, and reinforcement learning. Neither human-written verifiers nor human annotators can provide such verification at scale, so the field increasingly turns to vision-language models (VLMs) as judges of CUA trajectories. But a fundamental question has long gone unexamined: are these VLM judges reliable enough? To study it systematically, we introduce OSReward, a realistic, high-quality benchmark that evaluates VLM judges on CUA trajectories. The trajectories come from diverse agent backbones executing human-verified instructions across platforms, then rigorously labeled with ground-truth verdicts through multi-stage human annotation. Building on it, we derive OSReward-Hard, a challenge set concentrating genuinely hard cases, and OSReward-Multi for fine-grained efficiency and alignment scoring. The most comprehensive evaluation of VLM judges to date finds even state-of-the-art models fall short of an ideal judge, sharing a systematic leniency bias that mislabels failed runs as successes. The few reliable enough to trust are too expensive to run at scale, while affordable open models trail far behind. To close this gap, we construct and release OS-Shepherd-100K, an open corpus of reasoning-annotated trajectory judgments for the CUA community. On it, we train OS-Shepherd (9B and 35B), open reward models that supply low-cost, stable, and reliable reward signals, matching commercial judges at 30-60% lower cost than the frontier. Extensive analyses further inform the design of reliable CUA reward at scale. Our code, benchmark, dataset, and model checkpoints are available at https://os-copilot.github.io/OSReward-Home/.

</details>


### [265] [An Instrument to Evaluate Governance Proposals: AI Policy Analysis at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28094)
*Paulo Carvao,Claudio Mayrink Verdun,Isabel Adler,Jeffrey Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于AI治理政策评估的多维分析框架，通过经验驱动的评分标准（rubrics）系统性地呈现政策权衡与规范性假设，而非直接评判政策优劣。该框架采用混合方法（专家定性反馈+计算文本分析）构建评分标准，并基准测试了商业大语言模型与领域训练的评分校准模型在政策分析中的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI政策辩论往往陷入二元对立立场，掩盖了背后的权衡取舍和规范性假设，缺乏系统化、透明的评估工具来帮助政策制定者理解不同提案的深层含义和权衡。

Method: 采用混合方法：一方面整合领域专家（SME）的定性见解，另一方面结合计算文本分析来设计和验证政策属性评分标准（rubrics）；量化不同政策目标的相对权重，并通过比较可视化支持解释性和跨政策比较；同时将商业LLM的输出与领域训练的rubric校准模型进行基准对比。

Result: 该框架能够系统性地量化政策目标的侧重点，通过可视化支持跨政策比较和可解释性；领域训练的rubric校准模型可有效作为不同通用LLM的政策分析基准。

Conclusion: 该框架不评估政策有效性或可取性，而是聚焦于政策属性间的相关性和对齐度。通过明确分析假设（属性选择、rubric构建、权重方案），用户可自行判断框架的嵌入优先级是否与其自身规范性承诺一致。该方法是司法管辖区无关的，适用于政策制定者、分析师和研究人员应对复杂的AI治理环境。

Abstract: This paper introduces a policy analysis framework for systematic, transparent assessment of AI governance proposals in an evolving and contested regulatory landscape. AI policy debates often collapse into binary positions that obscure underlying tradeoffs and normative assumptions. The framework structures policy analysis around multiple policy attributes, allowing users to surface priorities and tensions without prescribing outcomes. We use a mixed-methods approach that integrates qualitative insights from subject matter experts with computational text analysis to inform the design of policy attribute rubrics. This quantifies the relative emphasis of different policy objectives and presents them through comparative visualizations that support interpretability and cross-policy comparison. The paper also examines the use of commercial LLMs for rubric-based policy analysis, benchmarking their outputs against a domain-trained rubric-calibrated model with explicitly defined analytical assumptions. Rather than assessing policy effectiveness or desirability, the framework focuses on relevance and alignment across attributes. By making analytical assumptions explicit, including attribute selection, rubric construction, and weighting schemes, the framework enables users to evaluate whether its embedded priorities align with the users' own normative commitments. The approach is jurisdiction-agnostic and intended to support policymakers, analysts, and researchers navigating complex AI governance environments.
  Contributions: (1) multidimensional policy assessment through empirically grounded rubrics that surface tradeoffs rather than resolving them; (2) a transparent hybrid methodology combining feedback from subject-matter experts with computational validation; and (3) use of domain-trained rubric-calibrated models as a benchmark for comparing different general-purpose large language models.

</details>


### [266] [AISPA: User-Centric System Prompt Auditing for Large Language Model Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28617)
*Xiangning Lin,Shenzhe Zhu,Shu Yang,Zhenyu Zhang,Haoqian Zhang,Yipeng Zhao,Chengxuan Qian,Tianwei Wang,Ziheng Zhang,Zhenlong Yuan,Dingcheng Wang,Juncheng Wu,Yuan Si,Jiaxin Liu,Baolong Bi,Robert Mahari,Tobin South,Dazza Greenwood,Zexue He,Rishi Bommasani,Sophia Kazinnik,Andreas Haupt,Samuele Marro,Erik Brynjolfsson,Alex Pentland,Jiaxin Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AISPA框架，用于系统审计商业AI产品中的系统提示词。作者使用该框架对88个商业AI产品中的3,249条指令进行了八维评估，发现系统提示词设计差异巨大、保护性指令普及但覆盖范围浅、提示词日趋增長但问题性指令仍普遍存在（约40%的产品含至少一条损害用户利益的指令）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统提示词是开发者配置的、控制基础模型行为的关键指令，广泛应用于商业AI产品中，但很少向公众或监管机构披露，形成了严重信任与问责缺口，亟需系统性的审计方法。

Method: 提出AISPA（人工智能系统提示词保障）框架，从八个对用户重要的维度评估系统提示词的特定组成部分；随后利用该框架对88个商业AI产品中的3,249条系统提示词指令进行审查和分类（保护性vs问题性）。

Result: 四项核心发现：(1) 系统提示词设计在不同产品和开发者间差异巨大（部分组织平均每产品含60+保护性指令，另一些不到5条）；(2) 保护性指令普及但覆盖浅，98.9%的产品含至少一条，但仅24%覆盖全部八个维度；(3) 系统提示词持续变长且更注重用户保护；(4) 问题性指令仍然普遍，约40%的产品含至少一条损害用户利益的指令，且保护性与问题性指令常共存于同一提示词中。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了商业AI系统提示词在透明度、标准化和独立监督方面的迫切需求，呼吁对系统提示词加强外部审视与治理。

Abstract: System prompts are instructions configured by developers to govern the behaviors of foundation models in AI applications. They are used throughout commercial AI products, but are rarely disclosed to the public or regulators, creating a serious trust and accountability gap in the wide deployment of AI systems. In this paper, we introduce Artificial Intelligence System Prompt Assurance (AISPA), a user-centric framework for systematically auditing system prompts in AI systems. AISPA examines specific parts of a system prompt and evaluates them along eight dimensions that matter to users. We then use this framework to review 3,249 instructions from system prompts in 88 commercial AI products, classifying each instruction as either protective (of users) or problematic. Our audit surfaces four core findings. First, system prompt design varies substantially across products and developers, with some organizations averaging over 60 protective instructions per product while others average fewer than 5. Second, protective instructions are widely adopted but shallow in scope: 98.9% of products contain at least one, yet only 24% cover all eight dimensions of the AISPA taxonomy. Third, system prompts have grown steadily longer and more protective of users, suggesting that user protection is becoming a more visible concern in commercial prompt design. Fourth, despite this progress, problematic instructions remain pervasive: roughly 40% of products contain at least one instruction that works against user interests, and protective and problematic instructions frequently coexist within the same prompt. Our findings highlight the need for greater transparency, standardization, and independent oversight for system prompts in commercial AI products.

</details>


### [267] [MIND: Lightweight and Effective Memory Injection Defense for LLM Agents via Intent-Aware Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28103)
*Dongyi Liu,Haixing He,Xiaobao Wu,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MIND（Memory Intent-Aware Neural Denoising），一个轻量级的记忆注入攻击防御框架。它利用意图感知的信息瓶颈机制，从初始用户意图与逐轮行为中提取紧凑表征，过滤冗余信息并保留攻击信号，从而以低计算开销检测恶意记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体依赖记忆增强，但容易受到记忆注入攻击：攻击者注入的恶意记忆会被智能体检索到，导致其偏离用户意图并使任务失败。现有防御方法要么计算成本高昂，要么在多轮上下文中存在信息冗余问题，亟需一种轻量且高效的防御方案。

Method: MIND框架包含三个关键部分：（1）初步分析发现良性轨迹与受污染轨迹在初始用户意图与后续行为之间呈现可区分的关系；（2）采用意图感知信息瓶颈（Intent-aware Information Bottleneck, IB）从初始意图和逐轮行为中提取紧凑的意图-行为表征，保留跨轮的意图相关攻击信号，同时过滤任务无关和重复的信息；（3）使用轻量级检测器从所得表征中识别恶意记忆。

Result: 大量实验表明MIND在降低攻击成功率的同时保持了任务准确性和推理效率。在ReAct-StrategyQA数据集上，MIND将平均ASR-r和ASR-a分别降低了55.4%和55.3%，同时平均准确率和延迟与未防御的智能体相当。

Conclusion: MIND成功实现了轻量级且有效的记忆注入攻击防御，通过意图感知的信息瓶颈机制解决了现有防御方法计算成本高和信息冗余的挑战，在安全性、任务性能和效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM-based agents are vulnerable to memory injection attacks: Agents may retrieve poisoned memory from attackers, which diverts their behavior from initial user intent and finally causes task failure. However, existing defense mechanisms either incur high computational cost or suffer from information redundancy in multi-turn contexts. To address these challenges, we propose Memory Intent-Aware Neural Denoising(MIND), a lightweight defense framework for memory injection attack. Our preliminary analysis reveals that benign and poisoned trajectories exhibit distinguishable relationships between the initial user intent and subsequent behavior. Building on this observation, MIND employs an intent-aware Information Bottleneck(IB) to extract compact intent--behavior representations from the initial intent and turn-level behavior. The IB preserves intent-relevant cross-turn attack signals while filtering task-irrelevant and repetitive information, and a lightweight detector identifies malicious memories from the resulting representations. As such, MIND mitigates information redundancy in multi-turn contexts while avoiding the overhead of repeated LLM auditing. Extensive experiments show that MIND reduces attack success rates while preserving task accuracy and inference efficiency. Notably, on ReAct-StrategyQA, MIND reduces mean ASR-r and ASR-a by 55.4% and 55.3%, respectively, while matching the undefended agent in average accuracy and latency.

</details>


### [268] [Beyond Rephrasing: Book-Level Organization Improves Synthetic Textbook Data for Mid-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28109)
*Jiawen Tao,Miao Peng,Yaoming Li,Xiaokun Yuan,Mengzhou Wu,Wenhan Yu,Guoan Wang,Nuo Chen,Tong Yang,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了合成教科书数据中"书籍级文档组织"这一因素对语言模型预训练的影响，提出了一个可扩展的合成流水线（聚类、规划目录、组装书籍），产出686K本教科书（32B token），并通过受控对比实验证明书籍级组织（而非仅内容或长度）能带来显著的性能提升（平均+1.09）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的合成教科书数据研究主要聚焦于生成内容的质量或局部改写风格对预训练的影响，而忽略了另一个关键维度：相关内容是否被组织成连贯的书籍级文档。作者认为这是一个尚未被充分研究的重要因素，因此希望通过受控实验来验证其价值。

Method: 构建了一个可扩展的合成流水线，包含四个步骤：(1) 从预训练语料库中检索源材料；(2) 将其聚类为专题单元；(3) 规划分层的目录结构；(4) 将基于源材料的各章节组装成完整书籍。该流水线产出了686K本教科书（32B tokens），覆盖15,000+学科。为隔离影响因素，设计了多个受控条件：Split条件（固定内容和token但将每章作为独立文档）、RandomConcat控制（拼接不同书籍的章节）以及Rephrase条件（仅独立改写检索文档，不做聚类/目录/组装）。

Result: 用该合成教科书语料替换中等训练混合中的自然书籍，下游性能平均提升+1.09。受控对比显示：Full相对Split的+1.02增益隔离了文档包装的作用；Full超过长度匹配的RandomConcat，排除了文档长度作为唯一解释；Full相对Rephrase的+1.17增益证明了结构化合成的价值。在Llama3-8B上，Full同样优于RandomConcat和自然书籍。

Conclusion: 书籍级组织是合成预训练数据设计中一个独立且有价值的维度。即使内容和token完全一致（Split条件下），将相关内容组织成连贯书籍结构仍能带来显著性能提升，这为未来合成预训练数据的生成与设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Synthetic textbook data has improved language model pre-training, but prior work largely treats the benefit as a property of generated content or local rewriting style. We study a different factor: whether related content is organized into coherent book-level documents. We contribute both a scalable synthesis pipeline and controlled evidence that this organization matters. The pipeline retrieves source material from a pre-training corpus, clusters it into topical units, plans hierarchical tables of contents, and assembles source-grounded sections into complete books (our Full setting), yielding 686K textbooks (32B tokens) across 15,000+ disciplines. Replacing natural books in a mid-training mix with this corpus improves downstream performance by +1.09 on average. Controlled comparisons then disentangle the relevant design factors. A content-matched Split condition holds generated text and tokens fixed but treats each section as an independent document; Full's +1.02 mean gain isolates document packaging. A length-matched RandomConcat control that joins sections from different books remains below Full, ruling out document length alone. A retrieval-pool-matched Rephrase condition independently rewrites individual retrieved documents under the same audience-by-style scheme, without clustering, TOC planning, or book assembly; Full's +1.17 gain demonstrates the value of structured synthesis. On Llama3-8B, Full likewise outperforms both RandomConcat and Natural Books, supporting book-level organization as a useful axis for synthetic pre-training data design.

</details>


### [269] [BlueprintRepair: Typed Local Edits for Failed Lean Proof Blueprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28110)
*Ruslan Khrulev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BlueprintRepair，一种基于蓝图的LLM定理证明修复接口，通过十种schema检查的局部操作让模型修改证明依赖图；并构建BlueprintTrace基准（142个受控失败及其完整修复轨迹），实验表明类型化编辑在成本效率上优于精确源码补丁和完整模块重写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的Lean证明系统日益将证明组织为蓝图（形式化语句的依赖图），但现有修复方式（源码补丁、模块重写）成本高且可能改变目标定理。需要一个更受约束、可验证且schema检查的修复接口，同时缺乏控制变量的修复基准来公平比较不同接口。

Method: 提出BlueprintRepair接口：通过十种schema-checked的局部操作让模型编辑蓝图依赖图（操作显式命名所编辑节点，防止目标定理被修改；Lean校验每次变更，接受修复必须声明其证明使用的所有蓝图引理）。构建BlueprintTrace基准（142个受控失败，含完整接受与拒绝修复轨迹）。在匹配源码、反馈、模型和预算下，对比类型化编辑、精确源码补丁与完整模块重写三种接口，每个状态和接口各跑一个episode。

Result: 使用DeepSeek-V4-Flash，三种接口解决的本地化失败数量几乎相同。类型化修复每个已解决状态成本最低（补丁为其1.30倍，重写为其2.06倍），且在每任务10000个完成token内达到几乎全部最终覆盖率，而两种自由形式接口明显落后。第二模型Qwen3.6-Flash解决的states较少，但类型化修复仍最便宜，在证明撰写state上领先，并重复了本地化模式。

Conclusion: 类型化蓝图修复在保持相同解决能力的前提下更具成本效益，能以更低token开销接近完全覆盖，说明受限的结构化编辑接口优于自由形式的源码操作，是LLM驱动的Lean证明修复中更可扩展的范式。

Abstract: LLM-based Lean proving systems increasingly organize a proof as a blueprint: a dependency graph of formal statements. We introduce BlueprintRepair, a repair interface that lets a model change this graph through ten schema-checked local operations. An operation names the node it edits, so the target theorem cannot be changed. Lean checks every applied change, and an accepted repair must declare every blueprint lemma its proof uses. We also construct BlueprintTrace, a benchmark of 142 controlled failures with complete accepted and rejected repair trajectories. We compare typed edits, exact source patches, and complete module rewrites under matched source, feedback, model, and budget, one episode per state and interface. With DeepSeek-V4-Flash, the three interfaces solve almost the same number of the benchmark's localized failures. Typed repair is the cheapest per solved state (patching is 1.30x as expensive, rewriting 2.06x), and within 10,000 completion tokens per task it reaches almost all of its final coverage, while both free-form interfaces are well behind. A second model, Qwen3.6-Flash, solves fewer states but keeps typed repair cheapest, puts it ahead on the proof-authoring states, and repeats the localized pattern.

</details>


### [270] [ConMem: Contribution-Aware Memory for Long-Horizon Manufacturing Inspection Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28126)
*Bingchen Liu,Yuanyuan Fang,Lei Liu,Guangyuan Dong,Xing Fu,Yuanyuan Gao,Shuyue Wei,Xin Li,Xiangtian Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConMem是一个面向钢铁设备长周期检查场景的贡献感知记忆框架，通过将检查日志分割为功能证据单元、用Shapley式估计评估每个单元对下游诊断的贡献，并在受限记忆预算下保留高价值证据，实现了76.0%的QA准确率，同时将输入token数减少88.2%、响应时间减少86.6%，并在实际部署中成功保留了跨越三个检查周期的早期密封磨损弱信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检索增强生成系统将历史检查日志视为静态语料库，未评估记录的诊断价值，无法报告早期风险。长时序钢铁设备检查需要在跨多个检查周期的异构记录上进行推理，因此需要一个能区分记忆贡献、保留高风险弱信号的框架。

Method: ConMem包含三个核心步骤：(1) 将检查日志分割为功能证据单元(functional evidence units)；(2) 通过Shapley式估计评估每个记忆单元对下游诊断的贡献值；(3) 在受限记忆预算下保留高贡献证据。整体支持人在回路的早期风险筛查系统。

Result: 在真实数据集上，ConMem达到76.0%的QA准确率，超过最强直接基线；相比8K上下文LLM基线，平均输入token数减少88.2%，响应时间减少86.6%。消融实验验证了功能角色感知分割和贡献估值在优先定位弱退化信号方面的有效性。实际部署确认ConMem在三个检查周期中保留了早期弱信号，提供了面向现场检查人员的早期密封磨损预警。

Conclusion: ConMem证明了结合贡献感知的记忆管理与LLM辅助诊断的有效性，能够在长周期设备检查中保留早期退化弱信号，实现及时的风险预警，为工业设备检查场景提供了一种高效且可部署的记忆管理方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon steel-equipment inspection requires reasoning over heterogeneous records accumulated across repeated inspection cycles. Existing retrieval-augmented generation systems treat historical logs as a static corpus and retain records without estimating their diagnostic value, failing to report early risk. To this end, we propose ConMem, a contribution-aware memory framework for LLM-assisted equipment inspection, supporting a human-in-the-loop early-risk screening system. Specifically, our ConMem first segments inspection logs into functional evidence units, then estimates each memory unit's contribution to downstream diagnosis through a Shapley-style estimation, and finally retains high-value evidence under a constrained memory budget. In experiments, we evaluate ConMem on real-world dataset and ConMem achieves 76.0% QA accuracy, exceeding the strongest directly comparable baseline. Relative to the naive 8K-context LLM baselines, it reduces the average number of input tokens by 88.2% and response time by 86.6%. Ablation studies also show that the functional-role-aware segmentation and contribution-based valuation are helping prioritize weak degradation signals for targeted field inspection. Practical deployments further confirm that ConMem retains the weak early signal across three inspection cycles, providing an early-stage seal-wear alert targeted for on-site inspectors.

</details>


### [271] [AgenticASR: Refining Speech Recognition in Real-World Scenarios via an Agentic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28175)
*Zixuan Jiang,Binghao Qiang,Jiaying Chi,Yanqiao Zhu,Kai Yu,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticASR框架和AASR-Bench基准，实现流式语音的"意图保留型干净转录"——通过ASR-Refiner双模块架构，在音频流到达时反复改写活动上下文，消除口误、重复、自我修正等不流利现象并统一书写形式，同时支持任意时长流式音频的持续输出与修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASR追求逐字转录的准确性，但逐字输出包含填充词、重复、错误开头和自我修正等不流利内容，这些内容增加阅读负担、模糊说话者最终意图，并向下游任务传播未解决或已放弃的内容。现有口语音转文字方法只能处理完整音频或转录文本，无法在后续语音改变前文语义解释时修订已生成的文本。

Method: 1) 将任务形式化为Agentic Speech Recognition（AgenticSR），即音频到干净文本的任务，要求去除不流利、解决自我修正、规范化书写形式并保持说话者最终意图。2) 提出AgenticASR架构，采用ASR-Refiner双模块设计：ASR模块负责语音识别，Refiner模块在音频到达时反复变换有界活动上下文并替换对应的输出跨度，实现任意时长流上的持续发射与修订。3) 构建AASR-Bench双语基准，包含细粒度原子评分标准（rubrics）。

Result: 在多个ASR前端下，AgenticASR在AASR-Bench上取得最高的系统得分。人-AI一致性研究表明基于评分标准的判断与独立专家评估一致。消融实验刻画了Refiner容量、上下文长度以及在线与离线推理之间的质量-延迟权衡。

Conclusion: 实验结果确立了AgenticASR作为在持续语音过程中进行意图保持型干净转录的实用框架。代码、AASR-Bench和演示将公开发布。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has achieved substantial gains in transcription accuracy, yet verbatim transcription does not necessarily produce readily usable text. It retains fillers, repetitions, false starts, and self-corrections that increase reading effort, obscure the speaker's final intent, and propagate unresolved or abandoned content to downstream tasks. Existing spoken-to-written methods process completed audio or transcripts but cannot revise emitted text when later speech changes how preceding content should be interpreted. We therefore formulate Agentic Speech Recognition (AgenticSR), an audio-to-clean-text task that removes disfluencies, resolves self-corrections, and normalizes written form while preserving the speaker's final intent. AgenticASR implements this task through an ASR--Refiner architecture that repeatedly transforms a bounded active context and replaces its corresponding output span as audio arrives. This enables continual emission and revision over streams of arbitrary duration. We also introduce AASR-Bench, a bilingual benchmark with fine-grained atomic rubrics. Across multiple ASR front ends, AgenticASR attains the highest AASR-Bench scores among evaluated systems. A human--AI agreement study shows that rubric-based judgments align with independent expert assessments. Ablations characterize Refiner capacity, context length, and the quality--latency trade-off between online and offline inference. Together, these results establish AgenticASR as a practical framework for intent-preserving clean transcription during ongoing speech. Code, AASR-Bench, and a demo will be released at https://github.com/AnXMuy/AgenticASR.

</details>


### [272] [Old Tricks, New Models: How Simple Image Transformations Break Modern AI-based Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28187)
*Marco Alecci,Francesco Marchiori,Iyiola Emmanuel Olatunji,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé,Jacques Klein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对三种商业多模态图像审核API进行了大规模黑盒鲁棒性评估，发现仅通过简单的、无需梯度的图像变换（如颜色反转、灰度化）即可绕过这些系统，导致内容从不安全被误判为安全，且不同数据集和危害类别下的鲁棒性差异显著，表明基于基础模型的审核API不能作为独立安全防线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统任务特定的内容审核分类器在策略覆盖和上下文理解方面存在局限，而基于大型基础模型的商业多模态审核API被宣传为更广泛、更强力的安全过滤器。然而，这种范式转变是否能带来更鲁棒的图像审核尚未得到充分验证，本文旨在以黑盒方式系统性评估三类主流商业图像审核服务的鲁棒性。

Method: 作者对三个成熟的商业图像审核服务进行了大规模黑盒评估。具体方法包括：跨多个提供商、数据集、危害类别、感知相似性约束和变换强度，评估七种简单且与模型无关的图像变换（包括颜色反转、灰度化等固定变换），这些变换无需梯度、替代模型或对目标系统的任何内部知识。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 三个商业审核服务均可被低成本图像变换轻松绕过；(2) 固定的简单变换（如颜色反转、灰度化）就能引发不安全到安全的判定翻转，同时图像内容对人类仍然可辨认；(3) 不同数据集和危害类别下的鲁棒性差异很大，多模态内容和自残类内容表现出尤为明显的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 用基于基础模型的API替代传统审核分类器本身并不能提供可靠的安全边界。此类系统必须在真实变换场景下进行评估，并作为多层审核流水线的一个组成部分部署，而非作为独立的安全过滤器。

Abstract: While automated content-moderation systems have become essential for screening harmful content at scale, conventional task-specific classifiers often provide limited policy cov- erage and contextual understanding. Recently, commercial multimodal moderation APIs built on large foundation models have been introduced with the promise of providing broader and more capable safety filters. In this work, we analyze whether this shift also yields more robust image moderation. We conduct a large-scale black-box evaluation on three established commercial image-moderation services and compare their robustness. By evaluating seven simple, model-agnostic image transformations across multiple providers, datasets, harm categories, perceptual-similarity constraints, and transformation intensities, we find that: (1) all three commercial services can be bypassed using inexpensive image transformations that require no gradients, surrogate models, or knowledge of the target system; (2) even fixed transformations such as color inversion and grayscale conversion induce unsafe-to-safe decision changes while preserving content that remains recognizable to humans; (3) their robustness varies substantially across datasets and harm categories, with multimodal content and self-harm exhibiting pronounced vulnerabilities. This yields the conclusion that replacing conventional moderation classifiers with foundation-model-based APIs does not, by itself, provide a reliable security boundary. Such systems must be evaluated under realistic transformations and deployed as one component of a layered moderation pipeline rather than as standalone safety filters.

</details>


### [273] [Qwen-UI-Agent Technical Report: Toward Next-Generation Real-World Centric Foundation GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28227)
*Hanzhang Zhou,Panrong Tong,Xu Zhang,Quyu Kong,Chenglin Cai,Tianyu Xia,Gongjie Zhang,Jianan Zhang,Long Li,Long Chen,Lei Wang,Gaole Dai,Pengxiang Li,Liangyu Chen,Yue Wang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Qwen-UI-Agent，一个面向真实世界的基础GUI智能体，覆盖移动端、电脑端、网页和DeepSearch环境，通过统一动作空间（GUI操作与CLI执行交错）、AutoResearch式数据飞轮、超长轨迹在线强化学习（100+轨迹轮、10000+并发环境）和轻量级harn层支持主动服务启动与跨设备有状态工作流。在移动端、电脑端和浏览器端多项基准测试中取得SOTA或与前沿模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动GUI智能体从学术设定走向真实世界使用，核心目标包括：在真实设备上可靠运行、跨平台执行工作流、GUI与CLI结合、长时程任务、主动服务启动、以及以最小人工代价自主提升能力。

Method: (1) 环境构建：融合多样化的sandbox环境与大规模真实设备移动运行时；(2) 统一动作空间：在单次模型turn内交错生成GUI操作与CLI执行，并输出批量化动作；(3) 数据飞轮：采用AutoResearch方式，由智能体自主构建任务和环境、诊断失败并规划后续迭代；(4) 在线RL：支持超过100个turn的长轨迹训练，使用10,000+并发环境加速rollout；(5) 轻量harness层：支持主动服务启动和移动端/电脑端跨设备有状态工作流。

Result: 移动端：MobileWorld 82.1%、MobileWorld-Real 92.2%、AndroidDaily 97.5%；电脑端：OSWorld-Verified 79.5%、OSWorld-v2 部分进度40.0%；浏览器端：WebArena 73.6%、GUI grounding ScreenSpot-Pro 81.5%。移动端达SOTA，电脑/浏览器任务与Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.6 Sol等前沿模型表现相当。

Conclusion: Qwen-UI-Agent证明了真实世界导向的GUI智能体设计是可行的：通过沙盒与真实设备结合、统一的GUI+CLI动作空间、自动化的数据飞轮和超长轨迹在线RL，能够在移动、电脑、浏览器等多环境中取得领先性能，为GUI智能体的落地应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: GUI agents have the potential to become a general purpose executor over existing digital devices. To advance them toward real-world use, we envision agents that operate reliably on real devices, execute workflows across platforms, combine GUI interaction with CLI execution, complete long-horizon tasks, proactively initiate useful services, and autonomously improve their capabilities with minimal human effort. Guided by this vision, we present Qwen-UI-Agent, a real-world centric foundation GUI agent spanning mobile, computer-use, web, and DeepSearch environments. Qwen-UI-Agent combines diverse sandbox environments with a large-scale real-device mobile runtime. Its unified action space interleaves GUI operations with CLI execution and generates batched actions in a single model turn. An AutoResearch-style data flywheel uses agents to construct tasks and environments, diagnose failures, and plan subsequent iterations. Online RL supports training on trajectories exceeding 100 turns, with over 10,000 concurrent environments accelerating rollout. A lightweight harness layer supports proactive service initiation and stateful workflows across mobile and computer.
  Across a broad suite of evaluations, Qwen-UI-Agent sets state-of-the-art performance on mobile-use benchmarks while delivering competitive performance on computer- and browser-use tasks against frontier models, including Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and GPT-5.6 Sol. On mobile use, it achieves 82.1% on MobileWorld, 92.2% on MobileWorld-Real, and 97.5% on AndroidDaily. On computer use, it achieves 79.5% on OSWorld-Verified and a 40.0% partial-progress score on OSWorld-v2. On browser use and GUI grounding, it achieves 73.6% on WebArena and 81.5% on ScreenSpot-Pro, respectively.

</details>


### [274] [AI and Authenticity in Islamic Research: A Critical Evaluation of Generative AI Reliability, Hallucination, and Source Fidelity in Quranic, Hadith, and Fiqh Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28237)
*Muhammad Sajjad Akbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究实证评估了六个主流生成式AI系统在伊斯兰知识领域的可靠性，通过50个开放式问题涵盖《古兰经》注释、圣训、教法学等领域，结果发现AI系统可作为辅助学习工具，但不应被视为宗教裁决或学术研究的权威来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穆斯林群体日益依赖生成式AI获取宗教指导、教义解释和法律裁决，然而缺乏关于这些AI系统是否提供真实、可验证且值得信赖的伊斯兰知识的实证证据，尤其是在高信任宗教情境下的可靠性尚未得到系统评估。

Method: 采用混合方法框架，对六个主流生成式AI系统进行测试，使用50个现实开放式伊斯兰问题（覆盖《古兰经》注释、圣训、教法学、伦理、牧灵建议及教法学派敏感议题），在澳大利亚和英国的真实条件下收集响应，分析领域准确性、引文验证、幻觉、教法学一致性、不确定性处理、来源溯源及地域差异。

Result: 研究回答了四个研究问题：AI在不同伊斯兰知识领域的准确性、幻觉和不完整引文程度、教法学分歧与教派多样性的处理一致性，以及整体可靠性。结果显示AI系统当前更适合作为入门伊斯兰学习的辅助工具。

Conclusion: 当前生成式AI系统在伊斯兰知识领域的定位应限于辅助性工具，用于入门学习；在宗教裁决和学术研究等场景中，必须对照经过认证的原始来源和合格学者的专业意见进行验证，不应将其视为权威依据。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly used by Muslims for religious guidance, Qur'anic interpretation, Hadith explanation, jurisprudential rulings, and Islamic education. Despite its growing adoption, there is limited empirical evidence on whether current AI systems provide authentic, verifiable, and trustworthy Islamic knowledge suitable for high-trust religious contexts. This study evaluates six leading generative AI systems using fifty realistic open-ended Islamic questions covering Qur'anic interpretation, Hadith, Fiqh, ethics, pastoral advice, and Madhhab-sensitive topics. Responses were collected under real-world conditions from participants in Australia and the United Kingdom and analysed using a mixed-method framework examining domain accuracy, citation verification, hallucinations, jurisprudential consistency, uncertainty handling, source provenance, and geographical variation.
  The study addresses four research questions: (1) How accurate and authentic are AI-generated responses across major Islamic knowledge domains? (2) To what extent do AI systems produce hallucinations, incomplete citations, or unverifiable religious references? (3) How consistently do models handle jurisprudential disagreement, Madhhab diversity, and uncertainty? (4) Are current AI systems sufficiently reliable for religious guidance, Islamic education, and scholarly research?
  Overall, current generative AI systems are valuable as assistive tools for introductory Islamic learning but should not be treated as authoritative sources for religious rulings or Islamic research without verification against authenticated primary sources and qualified scholarly expertise. This study provides one of the first comprehensive empirical evaluations of AI reliability within Islamic knowledge, offering practical guidance for researchers, educators, AI developers, and the wider Muslim community.

</details>


### [275] [Operationally Guided Placement-Aware Learning for Industrial Online 3D Bin Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28257)
*Dheeraj Poolavaram,Aanchal Rajesh Chugh,Sebastian Dorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPAL 是一个面向工业在线三维装箱问题（3D-BPP）的基于学习的框架，结合操作引导的空余最大空间生成器（OG-EMS）、基于 xLSTM 的放置编码器和掩码排名策略，实现了更高的空间利用率与稳定、紧凑、平衡的包装效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线三维装箱问题是物流和工业码垛中的长期挑战。现有基于学习的方法主要优化策略，而候选生成和表示仍主要由几何驱动，忽略了工业场景中对空间效率、稳定性、紧凑性和平衡性的操作需求。

Method: OPAL 包含三个核心组件：1) 操作引导的空余最大空间生成器（OG-EMS），在每个自由空间区域内评估多个锚点，优先选择低处、支撑良好、紧凑且空间多样的放置；2) 基于 xLSTM 的放置编码器，建模几何与操作候选属性之间的依赖关系；3) 轻量级循环核心，结合嵌入、当前物品和托盘状态来排序可行动作，并使用近端策略优化（PPO）训练掩码排名策略。

Result: 在 BED-BPP 基准上，OPAL 达到 0.49 的平均空间利用率，其中操作引导的候选生成贡献了 15.1% 的提升，学习得到的排名策略贡献了 6.3% 的提升，同时保持了鲁棒的推理时性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在在线 3D-BPP 中，不仅策略优化重要，候选生成和表示层面的操作引导同样关键，显著提升工业场景下的装填性能。

Abstract: The online three-dimensional bin packing problem (3D-BPP) is a longstanding challenge in logistics and industrial palletizing. Recent learning-based methods use a learned policy to select among feasible candidate placements. Performance depends on the candidate generator and representation, especially in industrial settings where packings must be space-efficient, stable, compact, and balanced. However, prior work has mainly optimized the policy, while candidate generation and representation remain largely geometry-driven. We address this gap with OPAL, an operationally guided placement-aware learning framework for industrial online 3D-BPP which combines an Operationally Guided Empty-Maximal-Space generator (OG-EMS), an operational representation for each candidate placement, and a masked ranking policy trained with proximal policy optimization. OG-EMS evaluates multiple anchors within each free-space region and prioritizes low, well-supported, compact, and spatially diverse placements. An xLSTM-based Placement Encoder models dependencies among geometric and operational candidate attributes, while a lightweight recurrent core combines the resulting embeddings with the current item and pallet state to rank feasible actions. On the BED-BPP benchmark, OPAL achieves a mean space utilization of 0.49, with improvements of 15.1% from operationally guided candidate generation and 6.3% from learned ranking, while maintaining robust inference-time performance.

</details>


### [276] [LLM-Guided Evolutionary Search for Constraint Model Reformulation to Improve Solver Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28268)
*Kostis Michailidis,Dimos Tsouros,Nguyen Dang,Tias Guns*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用大型语言模型（LLMs）自动化完成面向性能的约束模型重构（reformulation）。作者设计了一个受自动启发式设计（AHD）启发的进化框架，其中LLM作为变异算子提出候选重构方案，并通过基准测试验证。为解决现有上下文保留策略缺乏多样性的问题，本文提出Profile-Diverse Retention（PDR）策略，利用最大边际相关性（MMR）对实例级运行时向量进行去重，保留行为多样化的历史尝试。在八个CSPLib问题上的系统评估表明：迭代重构能带来显著留出（held-out）加速；保留多样化上下文的策略优于仅保留近期或最快尝试的策略；基于验证集的选择能进一步提升所有策略的留出加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化问题在工业应用中广泛存在，通常通过声明式约束模型编译到求解器求解。虽然LLM已经能从自然语言生成正确的约束模型，但即使正确的模型也可能因建模选择不当而求解代价高昂。因此，本文的核心动机是探索LLM能否自动完成面向性能的模型重构，即在不改变模型语义的前提下优化其求解效率。

Method: 本文提出一个进化式框架：LLM作为变异算子，基于用户定义的基线模型和上下文信息提出候选重构方案；每个候选方案通过求解器验证求解正确性并基准测试性能。研究比较了多种搜索策略，用于控制哪些先前的尝试、指令和测量反馈进入LLM提示词。现有策略主要基于近期性或性能优劣保留上下文，但缺乏多样性。为此作者引入Profile-Diverse Retention (PDR)，利用最大边际相关性（MMR）对实例级运行时向量进行选择，以保留行为上多样化的历史尝试。最终使用验证集进行模型选择。

Result: 在八个CSPLib基准问题上系统评估显示：（1）迭代重构能产生显著留出（held-out）速度提升；（2）保留多样化上下文的策略在留出性能上优于仅保留最近或最快尝试的策略；（3）基于验证集的最终模型选择能提升所有策略的留出加速效果。

Conclusion: LLM可以有效地自动化面向性能的约束模型重构，关键在于提示词上下文的多样性管理。PDR策略通过行为多样性保留，比传统的近期或性能导向保留策略更有效，而验证集选择进一步增强了泛化性。

Abstract: Combinatorial problems appear in numerous industrial applications. A common approach is to formulate these problems as declarative constraint models that can subsequently be compiled to and solved by a range of back-end solvers. Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can produce correct models from natural language, but even a correct model can be expensive to solve because performance remains sensitive to modelling choices. In this work, we investigate whether LLMs can automate performance-oriented model reformulation. Inspired by Automatic Heuristic Design (AHD), we use an evolutionary framework in which an LLM proposes candidate reformulations that are verified and benchmarked against the user-defined baseline model. We compare AHD-adapted search strategies that control which prior attempts, instructions, and measured feedback enter each prompt. Existing retention strategies prioritize recency or performance, but do not explicitly diversify the context. To cover this gap, we introduce Profile-Diverse Retention (PDR), which applies Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR) to instance-level runtime vectors to retain behaviourally diverse attempts. We systematically evaluate the strategies on eight CSPLib problems using validation-based final model selection. The results show that: (i) iterative reformulation can produce substantial held-out speedups; (ii) strategies that keep the retained context diverse outperform those that retain only recent or the fastest attempts; and (iii) validation-based selection improves the held-out speedup of every strategy.

</details>


### [277] [MemHarness: Memory Is Reconstructed, Not Replayed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28272)
*Rong Wu,Daocheng Fu,Licheng Wen,Xuemeng Yang,Shu Zou,Jianbiao Mei,Yuxin Wang,Hairong Zhang,Yu Yang,Tao Hu,Cong Zhang,Botian Shi,Pinlong Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemHarness框架，让LLM智能体基于当前状态主动重构而非机械重放检索到的过往经验，通过GRPO端到端训练实现记忆的上下文适应，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著超越纯RL和静态记忆基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强智能体将检索到的经验视为静态记录原样重放，忽略了存储经验的抽象普遍性与决策时具体状态之间的差距，常导致负迁移。而人类回忆过往经验时并非逐字复述，而是根据当前情境进行重组和调整。

Method: 提出MemHarness框架，在每个决策步骤中，统一策略模型基于当前状态批判和重构检索到的经验，生成与情境接地的指导信息后再行动。重构能力通过GRPO端到端训练自然涌现。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，MemHarness大幅优于纯RL和静态记忆增强基线，在分布外（OOD）场景中展现出强鲁棒性。分析表明重构目标不仅能防止负迁移，还能在训练期间作为潜在指导信号，从根本上提升智能体的内在推理能力。

Conclusion: 将记忆从"重放"范式转变为"重构"范式，使LLM智能体能够主动利用和适应过往经验，是提升决策性能和泛化鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Retrieving past experiences has become a common strategy to enhance large language model agents. However, most existing memory-augmented agents treat retrieved experiences as static records to be replayed verbatim, injecting them into the context regardless of whether they align with the agent's current situation. This ``replay'' paradigm ignores the gap between the abstract, general nature of stored experience and the concrete, ever-changing states encountered at decision time, frequently causing negative transfer. In contrast, humans rarely recall past experiences verbatim; instead, they reorganize and adapt retrieved memories to fit the present context. Inspired by this, we propose MemHarness, a framework that equips LLM agents to actively harness and reconstruct past experiences based on the present context. At each decision step, a unified policy model critiques and reconstructs the retrieved experience conditioned on the current state, producing context-grounded guidance before acting. This reconstructive ability emerges naturally through end-to-end training with GRPO. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show that MemHarness substantially outperforms pure RL and static memory-augmented baselines, demonstrating strong robustness in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. Furthermore, our analyses reveal that this reconstruction objective not only prevents negative transfer but also serves as latent guidance during training, fundamentally improving the agent's intrinsic reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [278] [Tycho: Active Abstraction with Programmatic World Models for ARC-AGI-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28287)
*Jens Lehmann,Andrei Aioanei,Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ARC-AGI-3这一交互式技能获取基准，将抽象推理转化为参数化渲染确定性摩尔机环境中的主动学习问题，并引入Tycho编码代理系统，通过区分可操作观察与中间动画帧来构建、测试、规划、修复或绕过可执行假设。在相同推理预算下，actor委托模型构建者的策略达到最高88.49的平均RHAE，GPT-5.6 Sol和Opus 5均达到100.00 RHAE并完成全部183关。论文提出"主动抽象"这一核心问题：从昂贵交互中生成可测试模型，并决策何时构建、修复、使用或绕过模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统抽象推理（如ARC-AGI）是静态的单步任务，而现实中智能体需要在未知环境中通过有限次交互推断规则、隐藏状态和目标。动作效率至关重要——每一步都有代价。现有方法缺乏对交互式、动态、不可先验知晓的游戏规则的建模能力，需要一种能够将交互历史结构化为可测试假设的智能代理框架。

Method: 将交互环境形式化为参数化渲染确定性摩尔机。提出Tycho系统，从结构化交互历史中分离可操作观察与中间动画帧、关卡完成帧和游戏结束帧。系统支持构建、测试、规划、修复或旁路自由形式的可执行假设。比较四种编排策略（actor请求委托给模型构建者等），在25个公开游戏中以Claude Opus 4.8在匹配推理预算下评估。

Result: actor-requested delegation策略获得最高平均RHAE 88.49；GPT-5.6 Sol和Opus 5在该策略下均达到100.00 RHAE并完成全部183个关卡，游戏平衡的首次运行人中位数排名为98.5和100.0。Opus 5比官方人类基线总计少用61%的计分动作。自动修复在验证失败后可产生更准确复现观测转移的模型，但RHAE仅83.07。

Conclusion: 模型精确复现观测转移（transition match）不等于识别目标或改进下一步动作。强玩法需要同时决策何时构建、修复、使用或绕过模型。这一联合问题被称为"主动抽象"：从代价高昂的交互中生成可测试模型，并判断获取或使用模型的成本是否值得。该工作为交互式抽象推理和模型驱动的智能体决策开辟了新方向。

Abstract: ARC-AGI-3 turns abstraction into an interactive problem of skill acquisition. A player must infer an unfamiliar game's rules, hidden state, and goal while maintaining action efficiency because every move counts. We formalize these environments as parameterized rendered deterministic Moore machines and introduce Tycho, a coding-agent system that constructs and uses game-specific models during interaction. Tycho separates actionable observations from intermediate animation, level-completion, and game-over frames. From this structured history, an agent can model, test, plan with, repair, or bypass a free-form executable hypothesis.
  In one matched public-set run per policy, we compare four orchestration policies on all 25 public games using Claude Opus 4.8 under matched inference budgets. Actor-requested delegation to a model builder obtains the highest observed mean Relative Human Action Efficiency (RHAE), 88.49. With this selected policy, GPT-5.6 Sol and Opus 5 both reach 100.00 RHAE and complete all 183 levels. Their game-balanced first-run human-replay midranks are 98.5 and 100.0. Opus 5 uses 61% fewer scored actions than the aggregate official human baselines.
  Automatic repair after verification failures produces models that reproduce observed transitions much more accurately, yet reaches only 83.07 RHAE. Transition match indicates whether a simulator reproduces observed dynamics, not whether it has identified the objective or improves the next action. Strong play also requires deciding when to construct, repair, use, or bypass a model. We call this joint problem active abstraction: generating a testable model from costly interaction and deciding when acquiring or using it is worth its cost.

</details>


### [279] [One Human, $N$ Agents: Audit-Budget Allocation for LLM Agent Fleets under Miscalibrated, Correlated Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28317)
*Cesare Zavattari,Alessandro Tommasi,Giuseppe Prencipe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在有限审计预算下，人类如何对N个LLM代理进行审计排序。通过两级高斯copula模型刻画置信度排名审计在对抗性误标定和误差相关条件下的有效性，发现存在一个误标定阈值δ*，超过该阈值后按置信度排名审计劣于随机审计。两个先验预期被颠覆：预算越紧δ*反而越高，跨家族相关性并非低——共享难度主导了谱系。实证发现五个开源LLM的置信度近乎恒定、操作上无用，点估计落在或超过翻转阈值；专有模型信息量高且低于阈值。文章给出了"空转"监督的定量判据，并在记录轨迹上回放策略验证了排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个审计员需要在每轮仅有的B次审计预算内（B远小于N）监督N个LLM代理，这些代理的自我报告置信度可能被对抗性误标定，且存在相关性错误。现有文献缺乏对"置信度排名审计"在何条件下会失效的理论刻画，需要精确定位从有效到有害的误标定临界点。

Method: 将预算化噪声检查建模为两级高斯copula（two-level Gaussian copula），在理论上推导误标定阈值δ*的解析表达式；随后在五个开源LLM和一个专有模型上进行实证校准，并通过在记录轨迹上回放不同审计策略来验证理论排序。

Result: ① δ*随预算缩小而上升（反直觉）；② 跨家族相关性不高，共享难度主导谱系；③ 五个开源LLM置信度近乎恒定（操作上无用），其点估计位于或超过翻转阈值（但置信区间穿越该阈值）；④ 专有模型置信度信息丰富且低于阈值；⑤ 提供空转监督（vacuous oversight）的定量判据；⑥ 轨迹回放验证了策略排序。

Conclusion: 置信度排名审计并非普遍优于随机审计，其有效性高度依赖模型置信度的标定质量。盲目依赖部分开源模型的自我报告置信度进行审计排序，可能产生比随机选择更差的监督效果；需要定量判据来识别"空转"监督情形。

Abstract: A single human must audit $N$ LLM agents under a budget of $B \ll N$ audits per round, guided by self-reported confidence that may be adversarially miscalibrated and by correlated errors. We model this as budgeted noisy inspection over a two-level Gaussian copula and locate the miscalibration threshold $δ^*$ past which confidence-ranked auditing is \emph{worse} than random. Two a-priori expectations reverse: $δ^*$ \emph{rises} as the budget shrinks, and cross-family correlation is not low---shared difficulty dominates lineage. Five open-weight LLMs show operationally useless (near-constant) confidence, point estimates at or beyond the flip though CIs straddle it; a proprietary model is informative and lands below it. We give a quantitative criterion for \emph{vacuous} oversight, and replaying policies on recorded traces confirms the ordering.

</details>


### [280] [PathView-Bench: Can Multimodal Large Language Models Achieve Fine-grained Multiscale Understanding of Pathology Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28318)
*Zongyi Chen,Yu Liang,Jie Lin,Liansheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PathVU是一个面向计算病理学的视觉锚定基准，用于细粒度、多尺度的视觉理解评测。该基准基于23个公共病理影像数据集构建，包含14个VQA任务、61,673张图像和308,070个样本，覆盖28个器官。通过程序化评分评估区域定位、视觉识别、数量估计、空间推理和上下文不足判断等能力，评测发现18个代表性MLLM在多尺度病理图像细粒度任务上存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理学多模态基准主要评估最终诊断答案、报告或字幕，无法深入判断模型是否真正理解病理推理和决策所需的多尺度视觉内容。因此需要一个新的基准来弥补这一评估空白。

Method: 从23个公共病理影像数据集出发，利用人工监督标签和空间标注构建PathVU基准。基准分为Region FOV（高分辨率局部区域）和Slide FOV（宏观全切片视图）两个视野，将原始标注转化为确定性任务目标，实现区域定位、视觉识别、数量估计、空间推理和上下文不足判断等能力的程序化评分。

Result: 在18个代表性通用型、医学领域和病理专科MLLM上的评估表明，即便先进的模型在跨多尺度病理图像的细粒度视觉任务上也存在显著不足。

Conclusion: PathVU为病理MLLM的开发与评估提供了可复现的基础，能明确量化模型的显式多尺度视觉理解能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to analyze pathology images. However, dominant multimodal benchmarks in pathology mainly score final diagnostic answers, captions, or reports. These evaluations provide limited insight into whether a model understands the multiscale visual content needed for pathology reasoning and decision-making. We introduce PathVU, a vision-anchored benchmark for fine-grained and multiscale visual understanding in computational pathology. Built from 23 public pathology imaging datasets with human-supervised labels and spatial annotations, PathVU evaluates MLLM understanding in two fields of view: Region FOV for high-resolution local regions and Slide FOV for macro whole-slide views. By converting raw annotations into deterministic task targets, PathVU enables programmatic scoring of region localization, visual recognition, quantity estimation, spatial reasoning, and insufficient-context judgment. The benchmark contains 14 VQA-style tasks, 61,673 images, and 308,070 samples across 28 organs and 7,253,526 annotations. Evaluating 18 representative general-purpose, medical-domain, and pathology-oriented MLLMs, we observe substantial limitations even in advanced models on fine-grained visual tasks across multiscale pathology images. PathVU provides a reproducible basis for developing and evaluating pathology MLLMs with explicit multiscale visual understanding.

</details>


### [281] [Paying for Honesty Without Knowing the Truth: Reputation-Penalty Design for LLM Marketplace Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28330)
